Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the elementary framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 906. The resulting operator achieves bound O(151/n^3).

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_906(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 906. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^151(S, F) is trivial. QED.

This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 221.5 (Spectral Property)

Statement: Let the elementary structure S satisfy the conditions of spectral property framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 549. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 145 and converges at rate O(1/n^1).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 549 is immediate from the spectral property property. For the inductive step, we decompose the elementary structure using the spectral property theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 145. Each subproblem has a solution by the spectral property fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
